◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."
◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (15/7/6), with 2 wave-4 fought, 14 contests survived, 4 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices.
◈ THE WAR PATH — A moment of respect, from something that rarely respects. THE HERALD came. Named and located — the war council knew its face. They faced The Loud Thing and lived to sit here. They faced The Eulogy and lived to sit here. Even now the wave-5 bar reads: blood your fighters (0 of 2 veterans).
The table notes 1 ascension from one village — its terms improve accordingly. Nobody has ever seen that before.
The table notes the overtime: 4 weeks kept waiting, 1 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −19.

📺 SYSTEM: "🔴 LIVE! the terror broadcast: WHAT EATING BUILT"
◈ THE FEARED — "Look what eating built." The footage plays unasked — the deer, the fire, the ones who came for you and didn't come back. Nobody at the table is comfortable. That is the point.
The table has read your ledger. The protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared; promises kept. The light road is open.
The time you stayed your hand. The table doesn't know what to do with it — which is its own kind of fear. Your people breathe.
Restraint, from you, is the most frightening thing in the room. Your people notice the restraint too.
You spend it — the shield, not the sword. The small envoy stares. Your people stand a little straighter. Protective terror: the table writes it down and doesn't understand it, which is the point.
◈ THE FEARED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE FEARED — "Show mercy."
The most frightening thing you ever did. The table doesn't know what to do with mercy from a predator. Neither do you, quite. But the audience weeps, and the galaxy learns that the feared one chose gentleness — and that choice echoes longer than any threat. The terror stays, pledged now: a shield over every village, forever.
You came to the Table as the Feared the hard way: one fight at a time, most of them for someone else.
Before dawn, you had walked the fence again. It was a good fence. You knew it did not need walking. You had needed to make sure.
◈ THE ARCHITECT — Fatima Hassan ascended through doors you built: relay. The galaxy will remember the god's name. The village will remember who built the door. Both are true. Neither is small.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
